Welcome to Wallper
A quick tour to help you set up live wallpapers and understand how Wallper behaves on your Mac.
onboarding-slide-1
Set Live Wallpapers
Browse the library, pick a video you like, and set it as a live wallpaper in just one click.
onboarding-slide-2
Screen Saver & Lock Screen
Optionally mirror your desktop wallpaper to the screen saver and Lock Screen for a seamless look. Available on macOS 26 and newer.
onboarding-slide-3
Battery Saving Mode
Wallpapers pause on their own when the battery gets low, and come back the moment you plug in.
onboarding-slide-4
Multi-Monitor Support
Use different videos on each display or sync the same wallpaper across all your monitors.
onboarding-slide-5
Smart Notifications
Get gentle reminders when wallpapers are paused or when Wallper switches to energy-saving mode.
onboarding-slide-6
Start App at Login
Enable launch at login so Wallper starts automatically and your wallpapers are always active.
onboarding-slide-7
You can always revisit these tips later in Settings.
